(() => {
  const key = "oai-epic-native-launch";
  let launch = null;
  try { launch = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(key) || "null"); } catch {}
  function save() {
    try {
      if (launch) sessionStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(launch));
      else sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
    } catch { launch = null; }
  }
  function capture() {
    const fragment = window.location.hash;
    if (!fragment) return;
    window.history.replaceState(window.history.state, "", "/epic/launch");
    const params = new URLSearchParams(fragment.slice(1));
    const snapshot = params.get("snapshot");
    const workspace = params.get("workspace_id");
    launch = Array.from(params).length === (workspace ? 2 : 1) && (!workspace || /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(workspace)) && snapshot &&
      /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{80,16384}={0,2}$/.test(snapshot)
      ? { snapshot, workspace, id: crypto.randomUUID(), messageId: null, draftId: null } : null;
    save();
  }
  if (window.location.pathname === "/epic/launch") capture();
  function pending() {
    return launch && !launch.draftId && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("epic_launch") === launch.id
      ? launch.snapshot : null;
  }
  window.__oaiEpicNativeLaunch = {
    get: () => launch?.snapshot ?? null,
    workspace: () => launch?.workspace ?? null,
    destination: () => launch ? "/?surface=work&epic_launch=" + launch.id : "/epic/launch",
    pending,
    ownsMessage: (messageId) => launch?.messageId === messageId,
    metadata: (messageId, draftId) => {
      if (!launch) return;
      if (draftId != null) {
        if (launch.draftId ? launch.draftId !== draftId : !pending()) return;
        launch.draftId = draftId;
      } else if (launch.messageId !== messageId) return;
      const changed = launch.messageId !== messageId;
      launch.messageId = messageId;
      save();
      if (changed) window.dispatchEvent(new Event("oai-epic-launch-changed"));
      return launch ? { epic_launch_snapshot: launch.snapshot } : undefined;
    },
    claimAttachmentNotification: (expected) => {
      if (!launch || launch.snapshot !== expected || launch.attachmentNotified) return false;
      launch.attachmentNotified = true;
      save();
      return true;
    },
    clear: (expected) => {
      if (launch?.snapshot === expected || launch?.messageId === expected) {
        launch = null;
        save();
        window.dispatchEvent(new Event("oai-epic-launch-changed"));
      }
    }
  };
  window.addEventListener("hashchange", () => {
    if (window.location.pathname !== "/epic/launch" || !window.location.hash) return;
    capture();
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event("oai-epic-launch-changed"));
  });
})();

EduSmart HR \u0026amp; Workforce Management Portal\\n\\nThe EduSmart HR Portal should be a **distinctive, fully integrated Human Resource Management System (HRMS)** designed specifically for Kenyan schools. It should manage the entire employee lifecycle, from recruitment and appointment to payroll coordination, performance appraisal, professional development, leave, retirement and exit.\\n\\nUnlike an ordinary staff register, it should serve as the central workforce management system for every department in a school, while synchronising with the existing EduSmart LMS modules and providing Executive Leadership with a clear view of staffing, performance, costs and compliance.\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"modern human resources management dashboard employee analytics clean enterprise web application UI\\\",\\\"school staff management dashboard timetable attendance leave management enterprise software interface\\\",\\\"modern employee self service portal leave requests payslip performance dashboard web UI\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"5:4\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"bento\\\"}\\n\\n## 1\\\\. The HR Portal structure\\n\\n## EduSmart HR \u0026amp; Workforce\\n\\nPeople • Departments • Payroll Coordination • Compliance • Performance\\n\\n---\\n\\n**HR Command Centre**\\n\\nWorkforce overview, alerts, staffing gaps and HR analytics.\\n\\n**Employee Records**\\n\\nStaff profiles, contracts, qualifications and service history.\\n\\n**Attendance \u0026amp; Leave**\\n\\nDuty rosters, leave approvals, absence and relief cover.\\n\\n**Payroll \u0026amp; Benefits**\\n\\nPayroll preparation, statutory deductions and benefits records.\\n\\n**Performance \u0026amp; Training**\\n\\nAppraisals, targets, CPD and professional development.\\n\\n**LMS Synchronisation**\\n\\nConnect HR to timetables, transport, finance and school operations.\\n\\nThe HR Portal should have its own dashboard, navigation, permissions and employee self-service interface, but use the same secure school login and shared platform infrastructure as EduSmart. It must not become a disconnected application or duplicate information already held in other modules.\\n\\n## 2\\\\. Complete HR modules and features\\n\\nThe following is the proposed production-grade feature set for EduSmart. Each module should be accessible according to the employee's role, school, department and assigned permissions.\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"employee human resources profile management software dashboard professional records interface\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**01\\\\. Employee Information Management**\\n\\n- Unique employee number and complete staff profile.\\n- National ID, KRA PIN, NSSF, SHA/SHIF and emergency contact records, where applicable.\\n- TSC registration number, teaching subjects, qualifications and verification status.\\n- Contracts, appointment letters, promotions, transfers and service history.\\n- Next of kin, dependants and authorised beneficiary records.\\n- Secure document storage, expiry reminders and employee self-service profile updates.\\n\\n---\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"human resources recruitment applicant tracking system interview pipeline dashboard software\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**02\\\\. Recruitment and Onboarding**\\n\\n- Advertise vacancies on the school's careers portal.\\n- Receive applications, CVs and supporting documents.\\n- Shortlisting, interview scheduling, interview scorecards and approval workflows.\\n- Reference checks and qualification verification.\\n- Offer letters, contract signing and onboarding checklists.\\n- Automatic creation of employee records and authorised user accounts after appointment approval.\\n- Probation tracking and confirmation reminders.\\n\\n---\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"employee attendance leave management calendar dashboard HR software interface\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**03\\\\. Attendance, Leave and Duty Management**\\n\\n- Staff check-in/check-out with approved attendance methods.\\n- Daily attendance registers, lateness, absence and overtime records.\\n- Annual, sick, maternity, paternity, compassionate and other applicable leave categories.\\n- Configurable leave entitlements, balances, applications and approval chains.\\n- Duty rosters, weekend duties, holiday programmes and relief staffing.\\n- Teacher substitution requests and absence alerts integrated with Timetable.\\n- Optional biometric or access-control integration, subject to privacy and security safeguards.\\n\\n---\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"payroll processing HR software dashboard salary deductions payslip financial summary interface\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**04\\\\. Payroll, Benefits and Compensation**\\n\\n- Salary scales, grades, job groups and approved salary structures.\\n- Basic salary, allowances, overtime, bonuses and authorised deductions.\\n- Monthly payroll preparation, review, approval and locking.\\n- PAYE, NSSF, SHIF, Affordable Housing Levy and other applicable statutory items.\\n- Pension, insurance, staff loans and benefits administration.\\n- Digital payslips, payroll summaries and payroll audit trails.\\n- Payroll exports, bank payment files where supported, and accounting integration.\\n- Separate payrolls for permanent, temporary, casual, contract and other staff categories.\\n\\n---\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"employee performance appraisal goals professional development dashboard HR management software\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**05\\\\. Performance Appraisal and Staff Development**\\n\\n- Annual, termly and probation appraisal cycles.\\n- Individual targets, departmental KPIs and review comments.\\n- Teacher lesson observation, professional development and appraisal evidence.\\n- Administrative, support and non-teaching staff performance frameworks.\\n- Self-assessment, supervisor review and moderation.\\n- Performance improvement plans and recognition records.\\n- CPD courses, workshops, certificates and training budgets.\\n- Separate records for school appraisal processes and official TSC processes.\\n\\n---\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"organizational chart workforce planning employee skills matrix HR analytics software\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**06\\\\. Organisation, Departments and Staffing**\\n\\n- School-wide organisational charts and reporting lines.\\n- Approved establishment versus actual staff complement.\\n- Vacant posts, workload distribution and staffing ratios.\\n- Department budgets, job descriptions and position control.\\n- Transfers between departments, campuses and schools.\\n- Skills matrices, succession planning and leadership development.\\n- Department-level attendance, costs and staffing reports.\\n\\n---\\n\\nimage_group{\\\"query\\\":[\\\"employee relations case management occupational safety compliance dashboard HR software\\\"],\\\"aspect_ratio\\\":\\\"4:5\\\",\\\"layout\\\":\\\"carousel\\\",\\\"num_per_query\\\":1}\\n\\n**07\\\\. Employee Relations, Welfare and Compliance**\\n\\n- Confidential grievance and disciplinary case management.\\n- Incident reporting, investigation stages and documented decisions.\\n- Workplace safety, risk assessments and training records.\\n- Occupational health and safety records with restricted access.\\n- Staff welfare, counselling referrals and employee assistance.\\n- Contract expiry, document renewal and policy acknowledgement reminders.\\n- Retirement planning, termination, clearance and final dues workflows.\\n- Whistleblowing and confidential reporting with controlled access.\\n\\n## 3\\\\. Specialised staffing for school departments\\n\\nEduSmart must not assume that every employee is a teacher. Every school should be able to create its own departments, employee categories, job titles, reporting lines and approval structures.\\n\\n| Department or workforce | Specialised HR functions |\\n|--------|--------|\\n| Teaching staff | Subject qualifications, TSC number, teaching load, lesson observations and timetable assignments |\\n| School leadership | Establishment planning, strategic KPIs, confidential reviews and leadership succession |\\n| Finance and procurement | Authorised finance roles, procurement responsibilities, segregation of duties and audit training |\\n| Human resources and administration | Recruitment, staff records, contracts, leave and policy management |\\n| ICT and digital learning | ICT certifications, system access approvals, training and technical duty rosters |\\n| Transport | Driver licences, PSV requirements where applicable, vehicle authorisations, steward assignments and training expiry alerts |\\n| Library and digital resource centre | Librarian roles, resource management responsibilities and training |\\n| Laboratory and science | Laboratory technician qualifications, safety training and authorised laboratory access |\\n| Sanatorium and health services | Relevant professional credentials, restricted health records and credential expiry alerts |\\n| Catering and lunch services | Food safety training, kitchen duty schedules, hygiene and occupational safety records |\\n| Security | Guard deployment, shift schedules, incident training and licence checks where applicable |\\n| Grounds, maintenance and facilities | Skills, equipment authorisations, maintenance duty rosters and work orders |\\n| Co-curricular and sports | Coaching qualifications, activity allocations, event duties and safeguarding training |\\n| Marketing and admissions | Recruitment support, school events, visitor-facing duties and campaign responsibilities |\\n| Boarding and student welfare | House-parent assignments, shift cover, safeguarding training and welfare responsibilities |\\n\\nDepartments must be configurable by each school. The school should be able to add a new department without requiring a software redesign.\\n\\n## 4\\\\. How HR will synchronise with every EduSmart sector\\n\\nThis is the defining feature of the portal: **one authorised staff record, connected to every relevant school operation.**\\n\\n## EduSmart HR Core\\n\\nEmployee identity • Department • Role • Status • Permissions\\n\\n### Required integration rules\\n\\n- **Timetable\\\\:** approved teacher assignments and availability feed into timetable planning. Leave or absence triggers an alert to authorised schedulers to arrange substitution.\\n- **Finance\\\\:** approved payroll totals, department budgets, staff costs and authorised expense allocations feed into the Finance Portal. Payroll approval must not automatically authorise payment.\\n- **Transport\\\\:** HR supplies verified staff identity, employment status and relevant qualifications. The Transport team manages fleet assignments, driver and steward deployment.\\n- **eLearning and Virtual Laboratory\\\\:** teacher status, approved teaching assignments and relevant roles determine access to learning-management and teaching tools.\\n- **Security and Administration\\\\:** approved joiner, transfer and exit events update access permissions. Disabling a departing employee's access must not erase the employee's historical records.\\n- **Communication\\\\:** staff notices, approval requests, policy updates and training reminders are delivered to the correct authorised recipients.\\n- **Executive Leadership\\\\:** dashboards aggregate staffing, leave, vacancy, training, appraisal and payroll-cost information, with detailed access restricted by role.\\n- **School Marketing and Public Portal\\\\:** approved staff profiles, designations and biographies can be published only when specifically authorised; private HR records remain internal.\\n\\nThese integrations should use shared employee identifiers, defined permissions and controlled data exchange, rather than independently editable copies of staff records.\\n\\n## 5\\\\. Kenyan HR compliance and statutory management\\n\\nThe portal should be built around Kenyan employment requirements, with a compliance engine that can be updated as regulations change. It should not hard-code tax rates and statutory deductions permanently into the application.\\n\\n**KRA and payroll taxation**\\n\\nSupport PAYE calculations, applicable reliefs, exemptions, employee tax declarations, payroll summaries and return-ready exports. KRA's October 2025 guidance addresses the application of reliefs and deductions following the Finance Act 2025. citeturn392704view0\\n\\nOfficial resource: urlKenya Revenue Authorityhttps://www.kra.go.ke/\\n\\n---\\n\\n**TSC teacher records**\\n\\nTrack registration numbers, qualification verification, professional development, employment history and relevant compliance reminders. TSC provides separate teacher registration, TPAD, T-PAY and HR services, so EduSmart should complement these systems rather than claim to replace them. citeturn325517search6turn325517search7\\n\\nOfficial resource: urlTeachers Service Commissionhttps://www.tsc.go.ke/\\n\\n---\\n\\n**Data protection and confidentiality**\\n\\nApply least-privilege access, secure document storage, access logs, retention schedules, controlled exports and processes for employee access and correction requests. Assess applicable ODPC registration and data-protection obligations, including safeguards for health and other sensitive records. citeturn392704view2turn392704view3\\n\\nOfficial resource: urlODPC guidance and regulationshttps://www.odpc.go.ke/guidelines-2/\\n\\n---\\n\\n**Employment and statutory obligations**\\n\\nMaintain configurable rules and records for contracts, leave, working hours, overtime, disciplinary procedures, workplace safety, statutory contributions and employee separation. Have Kenyan HR/payroll professionals validate the applicable rules before production use.\\n\\n## 6\\\\. HR access levels and approval controls\\n\\nThe portal needs different interfaces for different users. A department head should not automatically be able to see salaries or confidential disciplinary files simply because they supervise staff.\\n\\n| Role | Access and authority |\\n|--------|--------|\\n| EduSmart Super Admin | Platform configuration and tenant administration; no unrestricted access to school HR records by default |\\n| School Director / Executive Leadership | Workforce analytics, establishment, budgets and authorised confidential reports |\\n| HR Manager | Employee lifecycle, contracts, recruitment, leave administration and HR compliance |\\n| Payroll Officer | Payroll preparation, deductions and payroll reports, subject to permissions |\\n| Finance Manager | Approved payroll totals, cost centres, accounting and reconciliation |\\n| Head Teacher / Principal | School staff oversight, staffing requests and delegated approvals |\\n| Deputy Head / Dean of Studies | Teaching assignments, workload and approved absence or relief coordination |\\n| Department Head | Assigned department's staff, duties, appraisals and leave recommendations |\\n| Employee | Own profile, leave applications, payslips, training records and HR requests |\\n| Auditor / Compliance Officer | Read-only access to specifically authorised records and audit evidence |\\n\\nImportant controls\\\\:\\n\\n- Use maker-checker approvals for payroll, salary changes and other high-impact transactions.\\n- Keep confidential case records separate from ordinary staff profiles.\\n- Record who changed a record, when, and why.\\n- Enforce school and tenant isolation on the server, not just in the user interface.\\n- Support delegated approval during authorised absence.\\n- Require additional authentication for sensitive administrative actions where feasible.\\n\\n## 7\\\\. Advanced features that will make EduSmart HR distinctive\\n\\n**AI-powered HR assistant**\\n\\nAnswer policy questions using the school's approved HR handbook, draft job descriptions, summarise anonymised workforce trends and flag incomplete records. AI must not independently make dismissal, recruitment, disciplinary or salary decisions.\\n\\n**Intelligent workforce and timetable planner**\\n\\nForecast staffing gaps, identify timetable conflicts, suggest qualified substitute teachers and flag excessive workloads before schedules are finalised.\\n\\n**Mobile employee self-service**\\n\\nStaff can apply for leave, access approved payslips, update permitted profile details, submit requests, acknowledge policies and receive notifications on phones or computers.\\n\\n**Automated alerts and workflows**\\n\\nNotify authorised users of contract expiry, probation deadlines, upcoming leave, licence renewals, appraisal deadlines, training requirements and incomplete payroll checks.\\n\\n**Predictive workforce analytics**\\n\\nAnalyse staffing costs, turnover, vacancies, absence patterns and training gaps. Use explainable trends and human review rather than opaque employee-risk scores.\\n\\n## 8\\\\. Recommended HR dashboard\\n\\nThe landing dashboard should be clean, professional and responsive on desktop, tablet and mobile.\\n\\n## HR Command Centre\\n\\nSchool workforce overview • Illustrative dashboard\\n\\nTotal employees\\n\\n# —\\n\\nFrom active employee records\\n\\nPresent today\\n\\n# —\\n\\nFrom attendance records\\n\\nPending leave\\n\\n# —\\n\\nAwaiting approval\\n\\nOpen vacancies\\n\\n# —\\n\\nApproved, unfilled posts\\n\\n---\\n\\n**Priority action queue**\\n\\n**Pending approvals**\\n\\nLeave, recruitment, contract and payroll workflows\\n\\n**Compliance reminders**\\n\\nExpiring documents and upcoming deadlines\\n\\n**Staffing and substitution**\\n\\nUnfilled positions, absent staff and cover requests\\n\\nNo live employee data is connected to this illustrative design. Actual figures must be calculated from authorised records.\\n\\n## 9\\\\. How to implement this safely in the existing EduSmart LMS\\n\\nBecause your EduSmart application is still running through Google AI Studio and its backend/database architecture has not yet been established, the first step is to inspect the existing project—not build a separate HR application or assume a particular technology stack.\\n\\n1. **Inspect the existing application.** Identify its framework, routing, authentication, database, file storage, deployment configuration and current role/permission model.\\n2. **Define the HR data model.** Establish employee IDs, department structures, employment records, contracts, leave, attendance, payroll, appraisals, approval workflows and audit logs.\\n3. **Build the HR interface in staging.** Add the dedicated HR navigation and responsive dashboards while preserving the public landing page, existing login, modules and accounts.\\n4. **Integrate shared modules.** Connect Timetable, Finance, Transport, eLearning, Communication and Executive Leadership using defined permissions and documented data flows.\\n5. **Validate compliance and security.** Test Kenyan payroll calculations, tenant isolation, access restrictions, audit logs, backups, recovery and employee data privacy.\\n6. **Pilot with one school.** Use test employees and payroll scenarios first. Compare outputs with verified HR/payroll records before enabling live processing.\\n7. **Deploy with rollback protection.** Verify backups and restoration, complete security testing and obtain sign-off before any production migration.\\n\\n## 10\\\\. Your HR portal specification\\n\\nI recommend treating this as a dedicated EduSmart product module called **EduSmart HR \u0026amp; Workforce Management**, with three connected experiences\\\\:\\n\\n- **HR Command Centre\\\\:** for HR officers, payroll staff and school administrators.\\n- **Employee Self-Service Portal\\\\:** for every authorised employee.\\n- **Executive Workforce Intelligence\\\\:** for directors and school leadership.\\n\\nTo finalise the scope, there is one important decision: how should payroll work?\\n\\n1\\\\. Payroll approach\\n\\nFully integrated payroll calculations, statutory deductions, payslips and accounting exports.\\n\\nHR management with payroll data exports to an existing payroll system.\\n\\nBoth options, configurable separately for each school.\\n\\n---\\n\\n2\\\\. Workforce coverage\\n\\nAll teaching, administrative, professional, support and temporary staff.\\n\\nTeaching staff first, with other departments added later.\\n\\n---\\n\\n3\\\\. Implementation approach\\n\\nComplete HR portal specification and build in planned phases.\\n\\nBuild the employee records, leave, attendance and approvals first, then expand.\\n\\n[Confirm HR Portal Requirements]\\n\\nMy recommendation is the **both-options payroll model**, coverage for all school departments from the beginning, and a phased implementation that ultimately delivers the complete portal. This gives EduSmart a scalable HR product suitable for schools with different staffing structures and payroll arrangements.\\n\\n---\\n\\nIf you want, I can:\\n\\n- Design next-level HR dashboard How HR will synchronise with every EduSmart sector\",\"126\",\"This is the defining feature of the portal: \",\"127\",\"one authorised staff record, connected to every relevant school operation.\",\"128\",\"EduSmart HR Core\",\"129\",\"Employee identity • Department • Role • Status • Permissions\",\"130\",\"Required integration rules\",\"131\",\"Timetable:\",\"132\",\" approved teacher assignments and availability feed into timetable planning. Kenyan HR compliance and statutory management\",\"149\",\"The portal should be built around Kenyan employment requirements, with a compliance engine that can be updated as regulations change. It should not hard-code tax rates and statutory deductions permanently into the application.\",\"150\",\"KRA and payroll taxation\",\"151\",\"Support PAYE calculations, applicable reliefs, exemptions, employee tax declarations, payroll summaries and return-ready exports. KRA's October 2025 guidance addresses the application of reliefs and deductions following the Finance Act 2025. \",\"152\",[678],\"153\",\"154\",\"Official resource: \",\"155\",\"156\",\"157\",\"TSC teacher records\",\"158\",\"Track registration numbers, qualification verification, professional development, employment history and relevant compliance reminders. HR access levels and approval controls\",\"174\",\"The portal needs different interfaces for different users. Advanced features that will make EduSmart HR distinctive\",\"205\",\"AI-powered HR assistant\",\"206\",\"Answer policy questions using the school's approved HR handbook, draft job descriptions, summarise anonymised workforce trends and flag incomplete records. How to implement this safely in the existing EduSmart LMS\",\"240\",\"Because your EduSmart application is still running through Google AI Studio and its backend/database architecture has not yet been established, the first step is to inspect the existing project—not build a separate HR application or assume a particular technology stack.\",\"241\",\"Inspect the existing application.\",\"242\",\" Identify its framework, routing, authentication, database, file storage, deployment configuration and current role/permission model.\",\"243\",\"Define the HR data model.\",\"244\",\" Establish employee IDs, department structures, employment records, contracts, leave, attendance, payroll, appraisals, approval workflows and audit logs.\",\"245\",\"Build the HR interface in staging.\",\"246\",\" Add the dedicated HR navigation and responsive dashboards while preserving the public landing page, existing login, modules and accounts.\",\"247\",\"Integrate shared modules.\",\"248\",\" Connect Timetable, Finance, Transport, eLearning, Communication and Executive Leadership using defined permissions and documented data flows.\",\"249\",\"Validate compliance and security.\",\"250\",\" Test Kenyan payroll calculations, tenant isolation, access restrictions, audit logs, backups, recovery and employee data privacy.\",\"251\",\"Pilot with one school.\",\"252\",\" Use test employees and payroll scenarios first. Compare outputs with verified HR/payroll records before enabling live processing.\",\"253\",\"Deploy with rollback protection.\",\"254\",\" Verify backups and restoration, complete security testing and obtain sign-off before any production migration.\",\"255\",\"10. Your HR portal specification\",\"256\",\"I recommend treating this as a dedicated EduSmart product module called \",\"257\",\"EduSmart HR \u0026 Workforce Management\",\"258\",\", with three connected experiences:\",\"259\",\"HR Command Centre:\",\"260\",\" for HR officers, payroll staff and school administrators.\",\"261\",\"Employee Self-Service Portal:\",\"262\",\" for every authorised employee.\",\"263\",\"Executive Workforce Intelligence:\",\"264\",\" for directors and school leadership.\",\"265\",\"To finalise the scope, there is one important decision: how should payroll work?\",\"266\",\"1. Payroll approach\",\"267\",\"Fully integrated payroll calculations, statutory deductions, payslips and accounting exports.\",\"268\",\"HR management with payroll data exports to an existing payroll system.\",\"269\",\"Both options, configurable separately for each school.\",\"270\",\"2. It should manage the entire employee lifecycle, from recruitment and appointment to payroll coordination, performance appraisal, professional development, leave, retirement and exit.\\n\\nUnlike an ordinary staff register, it should serve as the central workforce management system for every department in a school, while synchronising with the existing EduSmart LMS modules and providing Executive Leadership with a clear view of staffing, performance, costs and compliance.\\n\\n\u003cAsyncImageGroup query={[\\\"modern human resources management dashboard employee analytics clean enterprise web application UI\\\",\\\"school staff management dashboard timetable attendance leave management enterprise software interface\\\",\\\"modern employee self service portal leave requests payslip performance dashboard web UI\\\"]} aspectRatio=\\\"5:4\\\" layout=\\\"bento\\\" size=\\\"lg\\\"/\u003e\\n\\n## 1. \u003c/grid\u003e\\n\u003c/box\u003e\\n\\nThe HR Portal should have its own dashboard, navigation, permissions and employee self-service interface, but use the same secure school login and shared platform infrastructure as EduSmart. It must not become a disconnected application or duplicate information already held in other modules.\\n\\n## 2. Complete HR modules and features\\n\\nThe following is the proposed production-grade feature set for EduSmart. Each module should be accessible according to the employee's role, school, department and assigned permissions.\\n\\n\u003cbox gap={3}\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"employee human resources profile management software dashboard professional records interface\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **01. Employee Information Management**\\n\\n      - Unique employee number and complete staff profile.\\n      - National ID, KRA PIN, NSSF, SHA/SHIF and emergency contact records, where applicable.\\n      - TSC registration number, teaching subjects, qualifications and verification status.\\n      - Contracts, appointment letters, promotions, transfers and service history.\\n      - Next of kin, dependants and authorised beneficiary records.\\n      - Secure document storage, expiry reminders and employee self-service profile updates.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"human resources recruitment applicant tracking system interview pipeline dashboard software\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **02. Recruitment and Onboarding**\\n\\n      - Advertise vacancies on the school's careers portal.\\n      - Receive applications, CVs and supporting documents.\\n      - Shortlisting, interview scheduling, interview scorecards and approval workflows.\\n      - Reference checks and qualification verification.\\n      - Offer letters, contract signing and onboarding checklists.\\n      - Automatic creation of employee records and authorised user accounts after appointment approval.\\n      - Probation tracking and confirmation reminders.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"employee attendance leave management calendar dashboard HR software interface\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **03. Attendance, Leave and Duty Management**\\n\\n      - Staff check-in/check-out with approved attendance methods.\\n      - Daily attendance registers, lateness, absence and overtime records.\\n      - Annual, sick, maternity, paternity, compassionate and other applicable leave categories.\\n      - Configurable leave entitlements, balances, applications and approval chains.\\n      - Duty rosters, weekend duties, holiday programmes and relief staffing.\\n      - Teacher substitution requests and absence alerts integrated with Timetable.\\n      - Optional biometric or access-control integration, subject to privacy and security safeguards.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"payroll processing HR software dashboard salary deductions payslip financial summary interface\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **04. Payroll, Benefits and Compensation**\\n\\n      - Salary scales, grades, job groups and approved salary structures.\\n      - Basic salary, allowances, overtime, bonuses and authorised deductions.\\n      - Monthly payroll preparation, review, approval and locking.\\n      - PAYE, NSSF, SHIF, Affordable Housing Levy and other applicable statutory items.\\n      - Pension, insurance, staff loans and benefits administration.\\n      - Digital payslips, payroll summaries and payroll audit trails.\\n      - Payroll exports, bank payment files where supported, and accounting integration.\\n      - Separate payrolls for permanent, temporary, casual, contract and other staff categories.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"employee performance appraisal goals professional development dashboard HR management software\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **05. Performance Appraisal and Staff Development**\\n\\n      - Annual, termly and probation appraisal cycles.\\n      - Individual targets, departmental KPIs and review comments.\\n      - Teacher lesson observation, professional development and appraisal evidence.\\n      - Administrative, support and non-teaching staff performance frameworks.\\n      - Self-assessment, supervisor review and moderation.\\n      - Performance improvement plans and recognition records.\\n      - CPD courses, workshops, certificates and training budgets.\\n      - Separate records for school appraisal processes and official TSC processes.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"organizational chart workforce planning employee skills matrix HR analytics software\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **06. Organisation, Departments and Staffing**\\n\\n      - School-wide organisational charts and reporting lines.\\n      - Approved establishment versus actual staff complement.\\n      - Vacant posts, workload distribution and staffing ratios.\\n      - Department budgets, job descriptions and position control.\\n      - Transfers between departments, campuses and schools.\\n      - Skills matrices, succession planning and leadership development.\\n      - Department-level attendance, costs and staffing reports.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cAsyncImage query=\\\"employee relations case management occupational safety compliance dashboard HR software\\\" aspectRatio=\\\"4:5\\\" maxWidth=\\\"112px\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **07. Employee Relations, Welfare and Compliance**\\n\\n      - Confidential grievance and disciplinary case management.\\n      - Incident reporting, investigation stages and documented decisions.\\n      - Workplace safety, risk assessments and training records.\\n      - Occupational health and safety records with restricted access.\\n      - Staff welfare, counselling referrals and employee assistance.\\n      - Contract expiry, document renewal and policy acknowledgement reminders.\\n      - Retirement planning, termination, clearance and final dues workflows.\\n      - Whistleblowing and confidential reporting with controlled access.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n\u003c/box\u003e\\n\\n## 3. Specialised staffing for school departments\\n\\nEduSmart must not assume that every employee is a teacher. Every school should be able to create its own departments, employee categories, job titles, reporting lines and approval structures.\\n\\n| Department or workforce | Specialised HR functions |\\n|---|---|\\n| Teaching staff | Subject qualifications, TSC number, teaching load, lesson observations and timetable assignments |\\n| School leadership | Establishment planning, strategic KPIs, confidential reviews and leadership succession |\\n| Finance and procurement | Authorised finance roles, procurement responsibilities, segregation of duties and audit training |\\n| Human resources and administration | Recruitment, staff records, contracts, leave and policy management |\\n| ICT and digital learning | ICT certifications, system access approvals, training and technical duty rosters |\\n| Transport | Driver licences, PSV requirements where applicable, vehicle authorisations, steward assignments and training expiry alerts |\\n| Library and digital resource centre | Librarian roles, resource management responsibilities and training |\\n| Laboratory and science | Laboratory technician qualifications, safety training and authorised laboratory access |\\n| Sanatorium and health services | Relevant professional credentials, restricted health records and credential expiry alerts |\\n| Catering and lunch services | Food safety training, kitchen duty schedules, hygiene and occupational safety records |\\n| Security | Guard deployment, shift schedules, incident training and licence checks where applicable |\\n| Grounds, maintenance and facilities | Skills, equipment authorisations, maintenance duty rosters and work orders |\\n| Co-curricular and sports | Coaching qualifications, activity allocations, event duties and safeguarding training |\\n| Marketing and admissions | Recruitment support, school events, visitor-facing duties and campaign responsibilities |\\n| Boarding and student welfare | House-parent assignments, shift cover, safeguarding training and welfare responsibilities |\\n\\nDepartments must be configurable by each school. The school should be able to add a new department without requiring a software redesign.\\n\\n## 4. How HR will synchronise with every EduSmart sector\\n\\nThis is the defining feature of the portal: **one authorised staff record, connected to every relevant school operation.**\\n\\n\u003cbox border radius=\\\"xl\\\" padding={3} gap={2} align=\\\"center\\\"\u003e\\n  \u003cbox background=\\\"#173B60\\\" theme=\\\"dark\\\" radius=\\\"lg\\\" padding={3} width=\\\"100%\\\" align=\\\"center\\\" \u003cgrid-item\u003e\\n        \u003cbox border radius=\\\"lg\\\" padding={3} gap={1}\u003e\\n          \u003cicon name={item.icon} size=\\\"lg\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n          **{item.name}**\\n          \u003ctext color=\\\"secondary\\\" size=\\\"sm\\\"\u003e{item.detail}\u003c/text\u003e\\n        \u003c/box\u003e\\n      \u003c/grid-item\u003e\\n    {/each}\\n  \u003c/grid\u003e\\n\u003c/box\u003e\\n\\n### Required integration rules\\n\\n- **Timetable:** approved teacher assignments and availability feed into timetable planning. Leave or absence triggers an alert to authorised schedulers to arrange substitution.\\n- **Finance:** approved payroll totals, department budgets, staff costs and authorised expense allocations feed into the Finance Portal. Payroll approval must not automatically authorise payment.\\n- **Transport:** HR supplies verified staff identity, employment status and relevant qualifications. The Transport team manages fleet assignments, driver and steward deployment.\\n- **eLearning and Virtual Laboratory:** teacher status, approved teaching assignments and relevant roles determine access to learning-management and teaching tools.\\n- **Security and Administration:** approved joiner, transfer and exit events update access permissions. Disabling a departing employee's access must not erase the employee's historical records.\\n- **Communication:** staff notices, approval requests, policy updates and training reminders are delivered to the correct authorised recipients.\\n- **Executive Leadership:** dashboards aggregate staffing, leave, vacancy, training, appraisal and payroll-cost information, with detailed access restricted by role.\\n- **School Marketing and Public Portal:** approved staff profiles, designations and biographies can be published only when specifically authorised; private HR records remain internal.\\n\\nThese integrations should use shared employee identifiers, defined permissions and controlled data exchange, rather than independently editable copies of staff records.\\n\\n## 5. Kenyan HR compliance and statutory management\\n\\nThe portal should be built around Kenyan employment requirements, with a compliance engine that can be updated as regulations change. It should not hard-code tax rates and statutory deductions permanently into the application.\\n\\n\u003cbox border radius=\\\"lg\\\" padding={3} gap={3}\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"landmark\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **KRA and payroll taxation**\\n\\n      Support PAYE calculations, applicable reliefs, exemptions, employee tax declarations, payroll summaries and return-ready exports. KRA's October 2025 guidance addresses the application of reliefs and deductions following the Finance Act 2025. \u003cCite refs={[\\\"turn392704view0\\\"]}/\u003e\\n\\n      \u003ctext color=\\\"secondary\\\" size=\\\"sm\\\"\u003eOfficial resource: \u003cLink url=\\\"https://www.kra.go.ke/\\\" title=\\\"Kenya Revenue Authority\\\"/\u003e\u003c/text\u003e\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"graduation-cap\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **TSC teacher records**\\n\\n      Track registration numbers, qualification verification, professional development, employment history and relevant compliance reminders. TSC provides separate teacher registration, TPAD, T-PAY and HR services, so EduSmart should complement these systems rather than claim to replace them. \u003cCite refs={[\\\"turn325517search6\\\",\\\"turn325517search7\\\"]}/\u003e\\n\\n      \u003ctext color=\\\"secondary\\\" size=\\\"sm\\\"\u003eOfficial resource: \u003cLink url=\\\"https://www.tsc.go.ke/\\\" title=\\\"Teachers Service Commission\\\"/\u003e\u003c/text\u003e\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"shield-check\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **Data protection and confidentiality**\\n\\n      Apply least-privilege access, secure document storage, access logs, retention schedules, controlled exports and processes for employee access and correction requests. Assess applicable ODPC registration and data-protection obligations, including safeguards for health and other sensitive records. \u003cCite refs={[\\\"turn392704view2\\\",\\\"turn392704view3\\\"]}/\u003e\\n\\n      \u003ctext color=\\\"secondary\\\" size=\\\"sm\\\"\u003eOfficial resource: \u003cLink url=\\\"https://www.odpc.go.ke/guidelines-2/\\\" title=\\\"ODPC guidance and regulations\\\"/\u003e\u003c/text\u003e\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003cdivider color=\\\"subtle\\\"/\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"file-check\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **Employment and statutory obligations**\\n\\n      Maintain configurable rules and records for contracts, leave, working hours, overtime, disciplinary procedures, workplace safety, statutory contributions and employee separation. Have Kenyan HR/payroll professionals validate the applicable rules before production use.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n\u003c/box\u003e\\n\\n## 6. HR access levels and approval controls\\n\\nThe portal needs different interfaces for different users. A department head should not automatically be able to see salaries or confidential disciplinary files simply because they supervise staff.\\n\\n| Role | Access and authority |\\n|---|---|\\n| EduSmart Super Admin | Platform configuration and tenant administration; no unrestricted access to school HR records by default |\\n| School Director / Executive Leadership | Workforce analytics, establishment, budgets and authorised confidential reports |\\n| HR Manager | Employee lifecycle, contracts, recruitment, leave administration and HR compliance |\\n| Payroll Officer | Payroll preparation, deductions and payroll reports, subject to permissions |\\n| Finance Manager | Approved payroll totals, cost centres, accounting and reconciliation |\\n| Head Teacher / Principal | School staff oversight, staffing requests and delegated approvals |\\n| Deputy Head / Dean of Studies | Teaching assignments, workload and approved absence or relief coordination |\\n| Department Head | Assigned department's staff, duties, appraisals and leave recommendations |\\n| Employee | Own profile, leave applications, payslips, training records and HR requests |\\n| Auditor / Compliance Officer | Read-only access to specifically authorised records and audit evidence |\\n\\nImportant controls:\\n\\n- Use maker-checker approvals for payroll, salary changes and other high-impact transactions.\\n- Keep confidential case records separate from ordinary staff profiles.\\n- Record who changed a record, when, and why.\\n- Enforce school and tenant isolation on the server, not just in the user interface.\\n- Support delegated approval during authorised absence.\\n- Require additional authentication for sensitive administrative actions where feasible.\\n\\n## 7. Advanced features that will make EduSmart HR distinctive\\n\\n\u003cbox gap={2}\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"brain-circuit\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **AI-powered HR assistant**\\n\\n      Answer policy questions using the school's approved HR handbook, draft job descriptions, summarise anonymised workforce trends and flag incomplete records. AI must not independently make dismissal, recruitment, disciplinary or salary decisions.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"calendar-sync\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **Intelligent workforce and timetable planner**\\n\\n      Forecast staffing gaps, identify timetable conflicts, suggest qualified substitute teachers and flag excessive workloads before schedules are finalised.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"smartphone\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **Mobile employee self-service**\\n\\n      Staff can apply for leave, access approved payslips, update permitted profile details, submit requests, acknowledge policies and receive notifications on phones or computers.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"bell-ring\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **Automated alerts and workflows**\\n\\n      Notify authorised users of contract expiry, probation deadlines, upcoming leave, licence renewals, appraisal deadlines, training requirements and incomplete payroll checks.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"start\\\" gap={3}\u003e\\n    \u003cicon name=\\\"chart-no-axes-combined\\\" size=\\\"xl\\\" color=\\\"#3975A5\\\"/\u003e\\n    \u003cbox flex=\\\"1\\\" gap={1}\u003e\\n      **Predictive workforce analytics**\\n\\n      Analyse staffing costs, turnover, vacancies, absence patterns and training gaps. Use explainable trends and human review rather than opaque employee-risk scores.\\n    \u003c/box\u003e\\n  \u003c/row\u003e\\n\u003c/box\u003e\\n\\n## 8. Recommended HR dashboard\\n\\nThe landing dashboard should be clean, professional and responsive on desktop, tablet and mobile.\\n\\n\u003cbox background=\\\"surface\\\" border radius=\\\"xl\\\" padding={3} gap={3}\u003e\\n  \u003crow align=\\\"center\\\" justify=\\\"between\\\"\u003e\\n    \u003cbox gap={1}\u003e\\n      \u003ctitle size=\\\"lg\\\"\u003eHR Command Centre\u003c/title\u003e\\n
